## Coverage

- all: 1896 identifier(s)

## Design system components

807 component(s) carry a stable `@uxid`. 34 declare variants (40 axis/axes, 111 value(s) in total), read from `cva()` calls, class maps, prop unions and conditional class branches.

19 of them are primitives under `lib/core/design_system`, identified by a `lib/core/design_system` directory of 21 files. 504 are application components, of which 161 are rendered in exactly one other file and 167 are rendered nowhere else (a page, or dead code).
